2025-06-14T21:24:31.3546885Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3547008Z <pr_title>
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3547450Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3548016Z </pr_title>
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3548182Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3548301Z <pr_description>
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3549082Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3549663Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3549728Z ## Problem
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3550005Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3550367Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3550602Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3550838Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3551067Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3551303Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3551517Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3551717Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3552100Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3552558Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3552630Z ## Solution
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3552722Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3552803Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3553087Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3553305Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3553943Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3555331Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3556316Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3556762Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3556903Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3557337Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3557613Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3557743Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3558053Z ```bash
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3558812Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3559657Z ```
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3559806Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3559931Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3560276Z ```bash
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3561206Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3562333Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3562704Z ```
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3562877Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3563121Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3563512Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3563669Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3564814Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3565637Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3565770Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3566171Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3567235Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3568082Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3568886Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3569778Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3570295Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3570901Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3571682Z 
2025-06-14T21:24:31.3571806Z Fixes #11.
